This page lists the IAM roles and permissions for Eventarc. To search through all roles and permissions, see the [role and permission index](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions) .

### Service agent roles

Service agent roles should only be granted to [service agents](https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/service-agents) .

<table>
<colgroup>
<col style="width: 50%" />
<col style="width: 50%" />
</colgroup>
<thead>
<tr class="header">
<th>Role</th>
<th>Permissions</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr class="odd">
<td>Eventarc Service Agent
<p>( <code>roles/ eventarc.serviceAgent</code> )</p>
<p>Gives Eventarc service account access to managed resources.</p>
<blockquote>
<strong>Warning:</strong> Do not grant service agent roles to any principals except <a href="https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/service-agents">service agents</a> .
</blockquote></td>

<p><code>container. serviceAccounts. delete</code></p>
<p><code>container.serviceAccounts.get</code></p>
<p><code>container.services.get</code></p>
<p><code>dns. networks. targetWithPeeringZone</code></p>

<p><code>iam. serviceAccounts. getOpenIdToken</code></p>
